6272 — dumbbell larsen press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6272 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Dumbbell bench press done with your legs held out in the air, so your chest and triceps do all the work without help from the lower body.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a bench with a dumbbell in each hand and lie back.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a bench with a dumbbell in each hand and lie back. 6272.json#/instructions/0
  2. Press the dumbbells up and lift your feet off the floor, legs extended. 6272.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower the weights to the sides of your chest with control. 6272.json#/instructions/2
  4. Press them back up until your arms are straight. 6272.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your core braced so your lower back does not arch. 6272.json#/instructions/4

6291 — warrior pose ii

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6291 after decompression) · Source record

A wide lunge with arms extended to the sides. It strengthens the legs and opens the hips.

Proposed staging

Starting Position
